# Hash crosswalk: private originals to public files

This package is a public copy of the author's private replication repository at commit `3f787f7c1769cf52473c6d626c94ba48065201e8`. Some files were changed before publication, so their sha256 no longer matches the value recorded in the private repository. The tables below map **every changed file** from its private sha256 to its published sha256: first the pinned files (a file whose sha256 is written in a manifest, a lock file or a document at that commit), then every other changed file; then the files added for the public copy.

- **No estimate changed.** Every panel frame and derived table in `data/frames/` and `data/report/frames/` is byte-identical to the private repository (only `README.md` and the economics event ledger differ: the ledger's evidence URLs are blanked and its `reason` and `evidence_source` notes are fixed English codes; no estimator reads either column), and `bash analysis/run_all.sh` re-runs the 23 local cells, each equal to its gold standard in `data/report/expected/` (ALL_EQUAL), and checks the chain of custody of the four cloud cells against the same gold standards (`CUSTODY_OK`, custody records in `data/report/custody/`).
- **Reasons used:** personal columns removed; evidence URLs cleared; names replaced by author ids; path made relative; internal process wording removed; Chinese comments and documentation translated to English (code verified unchanged); Chinese notes recoded to fixed English codes; renamed; fingerprint list regenerated; recorded sha256 values re-pointed (a report, test or document that records the sha256 of an edited file now records the published value; the private value is in this table).
- Every manifest verifies with `tools/verify_manifest.py` except the lines listed at the end of this file, which cannot verify here or in the private repository (products moved after their manifest was written, or never committed).
- The private originals, including the files not published, are planned for a restricted-access archive deposit (no DOI yet).

A further 2676 files of the private repository whose sha256 is not recorded anywhere are not published either (the same categories: personal data, internal records, private tools); `CHANGELOG.md` summarises them.

## Manifest lines that cannot verify (729)

Each of these lines is kept with its recorded path, here as in the private repository. **moved**: the manifest was written before its products were moved into the manifest's own directory; the line holds the published sha256 of the moved copy there (the file of the same name next to the manifest), which is how `analysis/paper/ledger_carried.py` reads it. **never committed**: the product was never committed (a large intermediate); its sha256 is as recorded. In the canon, a stage's own MANIFEST was copied with its stage directory: its paths are relative to the stage's output directory, so its lines are **moved** lines too. **packed**: the file ships xz-compressed next to its path (`<file>.xz`); once it is unpacked, the line verifies.
